# Phase-7 Wave 10 industry-specific repository joins — P7-INDUSTRY-JOINS-W10

## Scope and evidence boundary

This bounded tranche records page-9 unauthenticated public GitHub Search API repository metadata for the declared 17-industry/five-bucket universe. Waves 1–9 identities were excluded case-insensitively; one pre-repair Wave-10 duplicate canonical identity was reconciled by the deterministic first-seen rule documented below; no quota was padded. This is not an overall Phase-7 completion claim.

- Declared coverage universe: 17 industries / 85 buckets; emitted rows populate 15 industries / 39 bucket cells, while the other 2 industries / 46 bucket cells remain represented by underfilled-ledger records only.
- Wave-10 page-9 join records: 765; selected metadata-level joins: 334; held/unknown: 426; rejected: 5.
- Underfilled buckets: 54/85; quota-reached buckets: 31; no bucket was padded or forced.
- Unique canonical identities and records: 765 / 765; Waves 1–9 identity overlap: 0; Wave-10 duplicate records after reconciliation: 0.
- Discovery: 85 unauthenticated GitHub Search API page-9 queries, 93 HTTP attempts, 778 raw results, 8 rate-limit events preserved; 13 prior/duplicate results were excluded from the 765 emitted rows.
- Every row is reference-only, rights uncleared, SBOM unknown/not scanned, execution UNEXECUTED, and admission NOT_ADMITTED.

## Deterministic identity reconciliation

The pre-repair emission contained one duplicate canonical identity: `Tamhat/Construction-Management` appeared first as `P7-IJ-W10-0099` in Construction B01 and later as `P7-IJ-W10-0105` in Construction B03. The W10 rule is deterministic first-seen retention within the emitted row order: `P7-IJ-W10-0099` is retained and the later `P7-IJ-W10-0105` row is excluded from the repaired join stream. The B03 query receipt remains in `underfilled-bucket-ledger.jsonl` with `returned_count: 20`; the excluded record's API and repository URLs are preserved here: <https://api.github.com/repos/Tamhat/Construction-Management> and <https://github.com/Tamhat/Construction-Management>. No replacement was added. B03 therefore remains `underfilled` at 19 attempts / 18 selected / 1 held / 0 rejected / deficit 1.

## Quality gaps, contradictions, falsifiers and next gates

- GitHub Search API metadata is first-party public metadata evidence only. Search result descriptions and counters do not establish repository capability, adoption, production fitness, rights clearance, or implementation readiness.
- A declared SPDX license signal remains rights uncleared; NOASSERTION remains unknown. Sponsorship, contributor provenance, NOTICE obligations, dependency manifests and SBOM are unknown because no repository page, README, source or manifest was fetched.
- Archived/fork metadata is retained as reject; generic/thin/unresolved matches are held. Contradictions remain on each JSONL record and are not silently resolved.
- Falsifiers and next read-only clean-room gates remain per join row. Any future gate must stop before clone, source copy, execution, build, deployment, scan, benchmark or admission.
- Fresh CENA callback delivery is pending until the required herdr-2 pane readback; this lane does not claim overall Phase-7 completion.

## Boundary and verification contract

research_only=true; implementation_authorized=false; execution_status=UNEXECUTED; admission_status=NOT_ADMITTED; admitted_blocks=0; client/private data false; no login or credentials; no clone/copy/source execution/build/deploy/benchmark/scan; shared Phase-7 state unpromoted.
The lane smoke must recompute JSONL schema, exact counts, declared 17-industry/85-bucket coverage plus populated row cells, prior-wave overlap, duplicate identity, source URL/date/HTTP/rate-limit evidence, rights/SBOM boundary, Markdown links and scoped git diff before callback.
